Algorithm 1   
E<sub>xac</sub>t <sub>s ecu</sub>l<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>ve s</sub>i<sub>mu</sub>l<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>on o</sub>f <sub>one mar</sub>k<sub>e</sub>d CTMC bl<sub>oc</sub>k<sub>.</sub>   
Require: Initial state ${ \overline { { X _ { 0 } , } } }$ ti<sub>me</sub> �<sub>, cu</sub>t<sub>o</sub>f $\varepsilon ,$ d<sub>ep</sub>th �   
1<sub>:</sub> $m _ { 0 } \gets | A ( X _ { 0 } ) | , \quad T _ { 0 } \gets t ,$ � ← 0   
2<sub>:</sub> Dra<sub>w</sub> $E _ { j } \overset { \mathrm { i i d } } { \sim } \mathrm { E x p } ( 1 ) , j = 1 , \dots , m _ { 0 }$   
3<sub>:</sub> $T _ { j } \gets \bar { T _ { j - 1 } } \cdot \exp \bigl ( - E _ { j } / ( m _ { 0 } - j + 1 ) \bigr ) , j = 1 , \dots , m _ { 0 }$   
4<sub>:</sub> $N \gets | \{ j \in \{ 1 , \dots , m _ { 0 } \} : T _ { j } \geq \varepsilon \} |$   
5: while $n < N$ do   
6<sub>:</sub> � ← min $\{ K , N - n \} , \quad { \widehat { X } } _ { 0 } \gets X _ { n }$   
7<sub>:</sub> for $i = 1 , \ldots , L$ do   
8<sub>:</sub> F<sub>orm</sub> $D _ { i } \ \underset {  } { \mathrm { o n } } \mathcal { Z } ( \widehat { X } _ { i - 1 } ) ;$ d<sub>raw</sub> $\widehat { Z } _ { i } \sim D _ { i }$   
9: $\widehat { X } _ { i } \gets \Phi ( \widehat { X } _ { i - 1 } , \widehat { Z } _ { i } )$   
10<sub>:</sub> end for   
11<sub>:</sub> $P _ { i } \gets P _ { T _ { n + i } , \widehat X _ { i - 1 } } , i = 1 , . . . , L$ (batched)   
12<sub>:</sub> for $i = 1 , \ldots , L$ do   
13<sub>:</sub> D<sub>raw</sub> $U _ { i } \sim \underline { { \mathrm { U n i f } } } ( 0 , 1 )$   
14<sub>:</sub> if $U _ { i } \leq a _ { i } ( \widehat { Z } _ { i } )$ then   
15<sub>:</sub> $Z _ { n + i } \gets \widehat { Z } _ { i }$   
16<sub>:</sub> else   
17<sub>:</sub> D<sub>raw</sub> $Z _ { n + i } \sim R _ { i }$ (Equation (11))   
18<sub>:</sub> end if   
19<sub>:</sub> $X _ { n + i }  \Phi ( \widehat { X } _ { i - 1 } , Z _ { n + i } ) , \quad \ell  i$   
20<sub>:</sub> if $U _ { i } > a _ { i } ( \widehat { Z } _ { i } )$ then   
21<sub>:</sub> break   
22<sub>:</sub> end if   
23<sub>:</sub> end for   
24<sub>:</sub> $n \gets n + \ell$   
25: end while

We establish that SpecCTMC preserves the joint distribution <sub>o</sub>f <sub>even</sub>t ti<sub>mes, pos</sub>iti<sub>on-</sub>t<sub>o</sub>k<sub>en mar</sub>k<sub>s, an</sub>d <sub>resu</sub>lti<sub>ng s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>t<sub>es</sub> <sub>g</sub>enerated b<sub>y</sub> DirectCTMC. The ar<sub>g</sub>ument has two <sub>p</sub>arts: (1) <sub>correc</sub>ti<sub>ng</sub> <sub>a</sub> <sub>propose</sub>d <sub>mar</sub>k l<sub>eaves</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>even</sub>t<sub>-</sub>ti<sub>me</sub> di<sub>s</sub>t<sub>r</sub>ib<sub>u</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> unchan<sub>g</sub>ed, and (2) verification with residual correction <sub>pro</sub>d<sub>uces</sub> <sub>mar</sub>k<sub>s</sub> <sub>w</sub>ith th<sub>e</sub> t<sub>arge</sub>t <sub>con</sub>diti<sub>ona</sub>l di<sub>s</sub>t<sub>r</sub>ib<sub>u</sub>ti<sub>on.</sub> W<sub>e</sub> th<sub>en c</sub>h<sub>arac</sub>t<sub>er</sub>i<sub>ze</sub> th<sub>e num</sub>b<sub>er o</sub>f <sub>specu</sub>l<sub>a</sub>ti<sub>ve roun</sub>d<sub>s an</sub>d t<sub>arge</sub>t <sub>eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>ti<sub>ons,</sub> <sub>an</sub>d <sub>re</sub>l<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub> thi<sub>s</sub> <sub>cos</sub>t t<sub>o</sub> <sub>proposa</sub>l <sub>qua</sub>lit<sub>y.</sub>

## 5.1 Event-Time Sampling

Validity of event times sampled before their marks. <table><tr><td>Term or symbol</td><td>Meaning</td></tr><tr><td colspan="2">Process</td></tr><tr><td>DirectCTMC</td><td>Event-by-event exact simulation of the absorbing-mask CTMC: one target-model evaluation per unmasking event.</td></tr><tr><td>SpecCTMC</td><td>Exact speculative simulation of the same CTMC.</td></tr><tr><td>Natural event</td><td>One masked position unmasked at a random event time.</td></tr><tr><td>Mark  $z = ( d , \nu )$ </td><td>Position d and replacement token v of one event.</td></tr><tr><td>State update  $\Phi ( x , ( d , \nu ) )$ </td><td>Sequence equal to x except that position d is replaced by token v.</td></tr><tr><td>Product distribution  $Q _ { a } ^ { \otimes r }$ </td><td>Distribution of r independent binary pairs:  $\begin{array} { r } { Q _ { a } ^ { \otimes r } ( x ) = \prod _ { j = 1 } ^ { r } Q _ { a } ( x _ { 2 j - 1 } , x _ { 2 j } ) . } \end{array}$ </td></tr><tr><td>Masked positions  $A ( x ) ,$  count m(x)</td><td>Positions in state x equal to the mask token, and their number.</td></tr><tr><td>Target kernel  $P _ { t , x }$ </td><td>Conditional mark distribution at time t and state x: uniform position, model token</td></tr><tr><td>Exit intensity  $\lambda _ { t } ( x ) = m ( x ) / t$ </td><td>probability. Total event rate; depends only on time and mask count.</td></tr><tr><td>Cutoff  $\varepsilon ,$  deterministic completion</td><td>Simulation stops below model time  $\varepsilon ;$  remaining masks are then filled using a fixed rule.</td></tr><tr><td>Event-time sequence</td><td>Number and times of events at or above the cutoff, sampled before their marks.</td></tr><tr><td> $C = ( N , T _ { 1 } , \dots , T _ { N } )$  Greedy denoising</td><td>Deterministic finite-step decoder: confidence-ordered unmasking and argmax token.</td></tr><tr><td>Tau leaping, trapezoidal solver</td><td>Fixed-step approximate CTMC solvers of first and second order.</td></tr><tr><td colspan="2">Speculation</td></tr><tr><td>Speculation depth K</td><td>Maximum number of events drafted per round; d denotes a selected depth.</td></tr><tr><td>Round, draft length  $L _ { r }$ </td><td>One draft-evaluate-verify cycle and its number of drafted events.</td></tr><tr><td>Proposal  $D _ { i } ,$  draft  $\widehat { Z } _ { i } , \widehat { X } _ { i }$ </td><td>Proposal distribution, proposed mark, and tentative state at draft index</td></tr><tr><td>Cached target proposal</td><td> $i .$  Proposal that initially uses probabilities from an initial target-model evaluation and subsequently uses target probabilities evaluated at the most recently verified</td></tr><tr><td>Target row  $P _ { i }$ </td><td>tentative state. Target kernel evaluated at tentative pre-update state  $\widehat { X } _ { i - 1 } ;$  target rows are evaluated</td></tr><tr><td>Acceptance  $a _ { i } ( z ) , \alpha _ { i }$ </td><td>in batches. Maximal-coupling acceptance probability of mark z and its average over  $D _ { i } .$ </td></tr><tr><td>Residual  $R _ { i }$  Accepted prefix 11  $A _ { r } ,$  committed</td><td>Positive-part correction distribution sampled at the first rejection. Accepted events in round r and committed events including the corrected one.</td></tr><tr><td>Logical target rows W, charged</td><td>Work counts used in the analysis; rows used for initialization B, deterministic</td></tr><tr><td>rows  $\widetilde { W } ,$  evaluated states  $W _ { \mathrm { e v a l } }$ </td><td>completion F, and padding are counted separately.</td></tr><tr><td>Row consistency</td><td>Each batched target row equals the kernel of its own input state (Condition C6).</td></tr><tr><td colspan="2">Evaluation Metrics</td></tr><tr><td>NFE, sequential target-model</td><td>Target evaluation that must finish before the next state-dependent evaluation begins.</td></tr><tr><td>evaluation</td><td></td></tr><tr><td>NFE reduction  $R _ { \mathrm { N F E } } = N _ { D } / N _ { S }$ </td><td>Mean DirectCTMC NFE divided by mean SpecCTMC NFE. Fraction of drafted events accepted during verification.</td></tr><tr><td>Acceptance rate Wall-clock ratio</td><td>DirectCTMC total generation time divided by SpecCTMC total generation time;</td></tr><tr><td> $T _ { D } / T _ { S }$ </td><td>faster, parity, or slower by the paired 95% interval.</td></tr><tr><td>Per-evaluation cost factor  $E$ </td><td> $E = ( T _ { D } / N _ { D } ) / ( T _ { S } / N _ { S } )$  so  $T _ { D } / T _ { S } = R _ { \mathrm { N F E } } E .$ </td></tr><tr><td>Call cost  $C _ { d } ( n )$ </td><td>Time of one target call with d states at complete sequence length n.</td></tr><tr><td>Complete sequence length</td><td>Prompt plus generated tokens processed by each evaluation.</td></tr><tr><td>Target batch</td><td>Number of states per physical target call, specified by the experiment.</td></tr><tr><td>Mean accuracy at K, Pass @ K</td><td>Average correctness over K attempts; fraction of problems solved at least once in K</td></tr><tr><td>Accuracy ratio</td><td>attempts. Method accuracy divided by DirectCTMC accuracy.</td></tr></table>

## B.2 Masking schedule and time coordinate

L<sub>e</sub>t $\rho ( t )$ b<sub>e</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>pro</sub>b<sub>a</sub>bilit<sub>y</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>an</sub> <sub>answer</sub> <sub>pos</sub>iti<sub>on</sub> i<sub>s</sub> <sub>mas</sub>k<sub>e</sub>d <sub>a</sub>t <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l ti<sub>me</sub> �<sub>.</sub> A<sub>ssume</sub> th<sub>a</sub>t $\rho$ i<sub>s</sub> dif<sub>eren</sub>ti<sub>a</sub>bl<sub>e an</sub>d <sub>s</sub>t<sub>r</sub>i<sub>c</sub>tl<sub>y</sub> i<sub>ncreas</sub>i<sub>ng,</sub> <sub>an</sub>d th<sub>a</sub>t <sub>a</sub>ll <sub>answer</sub> <sub>pos</sub>iti<sub>ons</sub> <sub>use</sub> thi<sub>s</sub> <sub>a</sub>b<sub>sor</sub>bi<sub>ng-</sub> <sub>mas</sub>k <sub>sc</sub>h<sub>e</sub>d<sub>u</sub>l<sub>e.</sub> C<sub>on</sub>diti<sub>ona</sub>l <sub>on a pos</sub>iti<sub>on</sub> b<sub>e</sub>i<sub>ng mas</sub>k<sub>e</sub>d <sub>a</sub>t ti<sub>me</sub> $t ,$ it<sub>s pro</sub>b<sub>a</sub>bilit<sub>y o</sub>f b<sub>ecom</sub>i<sub>ng unmas</sub>k<sub>e</sub>d <sub>as</sub> ti<sub>me</sub> d<sub>ecreases</sub> t<sub>o</sub> $t - h$ i<sub>s</sub>

$$

## C.1 Total unmasking influence

Lemma 4 (Age of the cached target tensor). At the beginning ofevery round, the cached target tensor was evaluated either <sub>a</sub>t th<sub>e curren</sub>t <sub>comm</sub>itt<sub>e</sub>d <sub>s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e or a</sub>t <sub>an ac</sub>t<sub>ua</sub>l <sub>ances</sub>t<sub>or</sub> separatedfrom that state by one target-valid unmasking event. The cached tensor is the last row reached by verification, not a rowfrom the discarded sufix.

$$

d<sub>e</sub>fi<sub>ne</sub> th<sub>e</sub> <sub>exac</sub>t i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>egra</sub>t<sub>e</sub>d i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>ens</sub>it<sub>y</sub>

$$
\Lambda _ { n , i , \nu } = \int _ { I _ { n } } q _ { t } ( i , \nu \mid X _ { t } ) d t .\tag{66}
$$

Tau leaping. Tau leaping freezes the rate at the beginning of the interval (Cam<sub>p</sub>bell et al., 2022):

$$
\Lambda _ { n , i , \nu } \approx h _ { n } q _ { t _ { n } } ( i , \nu \mid X _ { t _ { n } } ) ,\tag{67}
$$

<sub>an</sub>d <sub>samp</sub>l<sub>es</sub>

$$
K _ { n , i , \nu } \sim \mathrm { P o i s s o n } \bigl ( h _ { n } q _ { t _ { n } } ( i , \nu \mid X _ { t _ { n } } ) \bigr ) .\tag{68}
$$

Second-order theta-trapezoidal solver. The trapezoidal <sub>me</sub>th<sub>o</sub>d <sub>uses a pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>t<sub>or an</sub>d <sub>correc</sub>ti<sub>on</sub> t<sub>o es</sub>ti<sub>ma</sub>t<sub>e</sub> h<sub>ow</sub> rates chan<sub>g</sub>e within the interval (Ren et al., 2025). The ex<sub>p</sub>er<sup>i</sup>ments use $\theta = 1 / 2$ <sub>.</sub> D<sub>e</sub>fi<sub>ne</sub>

$$
q _ { n } ( i , \nu ) = q _ { t _ { n } } ( i , \nu \mid X _ { t _ { n } } ) .\tag{70}
$$

Th<sub>e</sub> <sub>pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>t<sub>or</sub> <sub>samp</sub>l<sub>es</sub>

$$
K _ { n , i , \nu } ^ { \mathrm { p r e d } } \sim \mathrm { P o i s s o n } \left( \frac { h _ { n } } { 2 } q _ { n } ( i , \nu ) \right) ,\tag{71}
$$

<sub>w</sub>hi<sub>c</sub>h <sub>g</sub>i<sub>ves an</sub> i<sub>n</sub>t<sub>erme</sub>di<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e s</sub>t<sub>a</sub>t<sub>e</sub> $X ^ { * }$ <sub>.</sub> Th<sub>e secon</sub>d <sub>mo</sub>d<sub>e</sub>l <sub>eva</sub>l<sub>ua</sub>ti<sub>on g</sub>i<sub>ves</sub>

$$

$$

L<sub>e</sub>t $q _ { n } ^ { \mathrm { a l i g n e d } }$ <sub>equa</sub>l th<sub>e</sub> i<sub>n</sub>iti<sub>a</sub>l <sub>ra</sub>t<sub>e a</sub>t <sub>pos</sub>iti<sub>ons</sub> l<sub>e</sub>ft <sub>unc</sub>h<sub>ange</sub>d b<sub>y</sub> th<sub>e pre</sub>di<sub>c</sub>t<sub>or an</sub>d <sub>zero a</sub>t <sub>pos</sub>iti<sub>ons a</sub>l<sub>rea</sub>d<sub>y unmas</sub>k<sub>e</sub>d<sub>.</sub>

$$

<sub>w</sub>h<sub>ere</sub> $[ a ] _ { + } = \operatorname* { m a x } ( a , 0 ) . \mathrm { A t } \theta = 1 / 2 ,$

$$
q _ { n } ^ { \mathrm { c o r r } } = \left[ q _ { n } ^ { * } - \frac { 1 } { 2 } q _ { n } ^ { \mathrm { a l i g n e d } } \right] _ { + } .\tag{74}
$$

$$
